\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-1}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Remove transitivity:} two locally comparable steps no longer
  justify an earlier-than conclusion across a chain.
\item
  \textbf{Remove total comparison:} two distinct minimal selected
  children may remain incomparable, so uniqueness does not follow.
\item
  \textbf{Demand record equality:} proof-bearing slot records may encode
  the same selected child without being definitionally identical; the
  correct result is extensional child equality.
\item
  \textbf{Add least-open state selection:} the statement would require
  C005/C004 and create a backward dependency at node 007.
\item
  \textbf{Add Python agreement:} this would conflate a static theorem
  facade with the later executable selector owned by C004.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Infobox --- Order Before
Dynamics}\label{infobox-order-before-dynamics}

P002 demonstrates a deliberate separation: the canonical order of
admissible provenance continuations is closed first; only later does
C004 use an evolving born-prefix state to determine which admissible
continuation is actually the least currently open slot. Thus kinematic
provenance order is not conflated with state-dependent dynamics.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{008 · C004A --- First provenance slot
s₁}\label{c004a-first-provenance-slot-sux2081}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{008\ ·\ C004A}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C004A}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-4}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C004A}} is canonical node 008 and the first node of the bootstrap
group. Its verified hard predecessors are:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E005:\ C003\ -\textgreater{}\ C004A}}, supplying the finite
  b-ary provenance grammar;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E064:\ C018\ -\textgreater{}\ C004A}}, supplying the canonical
  breadth-first/lexicographic order.
\end{itemize}

The node must follow both predecessors. C003 alone gives the root, the
slot alphabet and address extension, but does not select a first
non-root address. C018 alone gives an order parameterized by a grammar,
but cannot identify a slot without the same C003 instance. C004A joins
these inputs and selects the first structural slot. The downstream edge
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E007:\ C004A\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}} is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and transfers the slot to the actual
first-birth construction.

\subsection{CNNA Architecture Role}\label{cnna-architecture-role-3}

C004A is the first explicit complement handoff: it selects the least
open continuation of the realized root but does not yet create a node or
relation. The possibility becomes realized only at C013.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-2}

Let \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{G}} be a C003 grammar with branching parameter \(b\ge2\),
root address \(\varepsilon\), local slot alphabet

\[
S_b=\{0,1,\ldots,b-1\},
\]

and finite cutoff \(L\ge0\). Let \(\Sigma\) be the C018 schedule
associated with the same grammar. The first provenance slot is the
triple

\[
s_1=\bigl(\varepsilon,0,\varepsilon\Vert(0)\bigr)
   =\bigl(\varepsilon,0,(0)\bigr).
\]

Its contract is:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  the parent is the C003 root \(\varepsilon\);
\item
  the stored local sibling rank is zero;
\item
  the intrinsic child address is \((0)\);
\item
  \((0)\) is the minimum non-root address under the C018 order;
\item
  the address is admitted into the finite approximant exactly when its
  depth one satisfies \(1\le L\);
\item
  the node performs no birth and carries no physical payload.
\end{enumerate}

The notation \(s_1\) is one-based ordinal prose. The internal C003/C018
rank is zero-based. Therefore \(s_1\) means
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{first\ slot\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{},\ not}}slot with
rank one'\,'.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-7}

The derivation requires an exceptional bootstrap before the recurrent
state exists. The root is already present, but there is not yet a
born-prefix state from which a recurrent least-open slot could be
selected. C004A therefore isolates the one structural slot that can be
selected without any prior non-root birth: the rank-zero child of the
root under the already-fixed C018 convention.

Keeping this selection separate from C013 is scientifically necessary.
The address \((0)\) is a provenance possibility. A birth additionally
requires finite-cutoff admission and later construction of a new carrier
state, directed relation and conductance data. Conflating those
operations would hide the exceptional bootstrap assumption inside an
address definition.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-2}

\subsubsection{Predecessor object}\label{predecessor-object}

The Python and Lean objects carry the same direct predecessor data:

\begin{verbatim}
grammar  : C003 FiniteBAryProvenanceGrammar
schedule : C018 CanonicalBirthSchedule
coherence: schedule.grammar = grammar
\end{verbatim}

Python checks the coherence dynamically in \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\_\_post\_init\_\_}}.
Lean stores it as the proof field \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{schedule\_grammar}}.

\subsubsection{Derived rank}\label{derived-rank}

The active domain \(b\ge2\) guarantees that zero is a valid local slot.
Lean internalizes this by constructing

\begin{verbatim}
firstRank S : Fin S.grammar.branching.value
\end{verbatim}

with value zero and a proof that \(0<b\). Python obtains the same value
from the first element of the exact tuple
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{grammar.slots\ =\ (0,...,b-1)}}.

\subsubsection{Derived parent and
address}\label{derived-parent-and-address}

The parent is not stored independently:

\[
\operatorname{parentAddress}(s_1)=\varepsilon.
\]

The address is C003 right extension:

\[
\operatorname{address}(s_1)
 =\operatorname{snoc}(\varepsilon,0)
 =(0).
\]

This construction is intrinsic and does not consult \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{L}}.

\subsubsection{Cutoff gate}\label{cutoff-gate}

Finite admission is the proposition

\[
\operatorname{WithinCutoff}(s_1)
\iff
|\operatorname{address}(s_1)|\le L.
\]

Because the depth is exactly one, positive cutoffs admit the slot and
zero cutoff rejects it. Python exposes the Boolean
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{admitted\_by\_cutoff}} and the partial operation
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{require\_admitted\_address}}; Lean exposes the proposition
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{WithinCutoff}} and separate proofs for the positive and zero
cases.

Parent, rank, address and admission are derived facts. They cannot be
supplied as mutually inconsistent constructor fields.

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-2}

The sources establish canonicity at three levels.

\subsubsection{Local-rank canonicity}\label{local-rank-canonicity}

For every local slot \(r\in S_b\),

\[
r=0\quad\lor\quad 0<r.
\]

Lean theorem \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{firstRank\_eq\_or\_before}} expresses exactly this
dichotomy as equality with \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{firstRank}} or strict C018 slot
precedence.

\subsubsection{Address canonicity}\label{address-canonicity}

For every non-root address \(c\),

\[
c=(0)\quad\lor\quad(0)\prec_{\mathrm{BFSlex}}c.
\]

The proof splits the address syntax:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  the empty word contradicts non-rootness;
\item
  a depth-one word is either rank zero or a later sibling;
\item
  a word of depth at least two follows \((0)\) by breadth-first depth
  priority.
\end{itemize}

Together with C018 irreflexivity, this makes \((0)\) the unique minimum
non-root address.

\subsubsection{Constructor canonicity
boundary}\label{constructor-canonicity-boundary}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{build\_canonical\_first\_provenance\_slot}} and Lean
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{build}} use the active C018 constructor. The formal source does
not separately state a theorem that all inhabitants of the
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FirstProvenanceSlot}} structure are definitionally equal. The
proved scientific claim is the canonicity of the extracted
parent/rank/address data under coherent C003/C018 predecessors, not an
overstrong global structure-equality theorem.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-2}

\subsubsection{Cutoff L = 0}\label{cutoff-l-0}

The C003 intrinsic word \((0)\) exists, and C004A still computes the
same structural slot. However:

\[
\mathcal A_{b,0}=\{\varepsilon\},
\qquad
(0)\notin\mathcal A_{b,0}.
\]

Python therefore has \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{schedule.slots\ ==\ ()}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{admitted\_by\_cutoff\ ==\ False}}, and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{require\_admitted\_address()}} raises \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ValueError}}. Lean
theorem \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{notWithinCutoff\_at\_zero}} proves the same exclusion
proposition. No first non-root birth is possible at \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{L=0}}.

\subsubsection{Positive cutoff}\label{positive-cutoff}

For every tested and proved \(L\ge1\), \((0)\) is admitted. This gate
only returns or proves an admissible address; it does not perform C013.

\subsubsection{One-based name versus zero-based
rank}\label{one-based-name-versus-zero-based-rank}

Replacing rank zero by rank one would select the second child and
violate both C018 minimality and the source tests. The notation and
stored rank must therefore remain distinct.

\subsubsection{Incoherent predecessors}\label{incoherent-predecessors}

A grammar paired with a schedule based on another grammar is rejected.
Without this condition, the rank/address projection could be interpreted
in a different branching or cutoff context from the schedule order.

\subsubsection{Active branching domain}\label{active-branching-domain}

The proof uses the project domain \(b\ge2\). The existence of rank zero
would require only a nonempty alphabet, but C004A does not enlarge or
alter the active CNNA input domain fixed by I001.

independently of cutoff. Lean encodes the structural side directly and
treats finite admission as a proposition. This is a representation
difference, not a semantic mismatch.

The nontrivial Lean chain is:

\begin{verbatim}
firstRank_eq_or_before
  -> address_before_distinct_root_sibling

address_depth
  + firstRank_eq_or_before
  + C018.shallower_before
  -> address_eq_or_before_nonroot
  -> address_before_distinct_nonroot

address_depth
  -> withinCutoff_of_one_le
  -> notWithinCutoff_at_zero
\end{verbatim}

The first chain proves local minimality, the second upgrades it to all
non-root addresses, and the third isolates finite-cutoff admissibility.
These are logically distinct obligations.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-7}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C004A}} closes the statement:

\begin{quote}
Given a C003 grammar and its coherent C018 schedule, the canonical first
structural provenance slot has root parent, zero-based rank zero and
address \((0)\). This address is the unique minimum non-root address
under the C018 order and belongs to the finite approximant exactly when
\(L\ge1\).
\end{quote}

The result is structural. It does not create the first non-root carrier
element or any directed relation.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-7}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E005:\ C003\ -\textgreater{}\ C004A}} supplies the address
  grammar and root.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E064:\ C018\ -\textgreater{}\ C004A}} supplies the strict
  schedule order.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E007:\ C004A\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}} is
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and supplies the first slot to the first
  non-root birth construction.
\end{itemize}

C013 additionally consumes A001 and N001 and requires cutoff admission.
It owns the newborn vertex, the first directed relation and the initial
conductances. C004A owns none of those objects.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{009 · A001 --- Genesis seed ★}\label{a001-genesis-seed}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{009\ ·\ A001}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{A001}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.2}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D0}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-5}

A001 is an auxiliary graph root with no hard predecessor. It is placed
after C004A in the reading order because C013 is the first construction
that needs an explicit bootstrap argument. This placement does not mean
that A001 is derived from C004A. Verified edge
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E006:\ A001\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}} is its only direct hard
handoff in the bootstrap block.

\subsection{Definition Or Statement}\label{definition-or-statement-3}

The seed carrier is the singleton set

\[
\mathcal S_\star=\{\star\}.
\]

The element \(\star\) has no numerical value and no address, parent,
child, relation, conductance, response, birth time, event index,
geometry or dynamical field. A001 is an explicit constructor token, not
a free CNNA model input and not a physical initial state.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-8}

The first non-root birth is exceptional: before the first relation
exists there is no nontrivial response network from which a recurrent
birth law can be evaluated. An explicit bootstrap argument makes that
exceptional constructor visible instead of hiding it in a null value or
an undocumented special case. The argument must be information-free so
that it cannot smuggle a third parameter into the derivation.

\subsection{Construction Or Encoding}\label{construction-or-encoding-3}

Python uses a frozen, slotted, zero-field dataclass \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{GenesisSeed}}
and the canonical instance \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{GENESIS\_SEED}}. Python can allocate
distinct object identities, but every instance has the same empty
dataclass value and therefore compares equal.

Lean uses the one-constructor inductive type

\begin{verbatim}
GenesisSeed.star
\end{verbatim}

with \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{GenesisSeed.canonical\ :=\ .star}}. The theorem
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{eqCanonical}} proves by constructor elimination that every value
equals the canonical token. Python and Lean therefore implement the same
singleton carrier while using language-appropriate object mechanics.

\subsection{Boundary Case Or
Countercheck}\label{boundary-case-or-countercheck-3}

The node-local Python test checks two independent boundaries:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{dataclasses.fields(GenesisSeed)\ ==\ ()}}, so no hidden payload
  exists;
\item
  the canonical token lacks every downstream result-like attribute
  listed by the test.
\end{enumerate}

A type with one constructor but a payload field would fail the A001
contract. Likewise, a numerical sentinel such as \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{0}} would be
semantically weaker because it belongs to a larger carrier and invites
accidental arithmetic use. The singleton type prevents both failure
modes.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-8}

A001 closes the existence of exactly one explicit bootstrap token and
proves at the type level that the token contains no choice. It does not
prove that a downstream constructor ignores the token.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-8}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E006:\ A001\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}} is
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and supplies the information-free token.
\item
  T001 later proves equality of the generated first-birth states for
  arbitrary explicit seed arguments.
\end{itemize}

\section{010 · N001 --- Initial conductance normalization
C★=1}\label{n001-initial-conductance-normalization-c1}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{010\ ·\ N001}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{N001}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.3}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D0}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-6}

N001 is a fixed-normalization graph root. It is introduced immediately
before C013 because the first directed relation requires an initial
conductance representative. It is independent of A001 and C004A. Its
hard outgoing edges are \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E008:\ N001\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E010:\ N001\ -\textgreater{}\ M005}}.

\subsection{Definition Or Statement}\label{definition-or-statement-4}

N001 fixes the dimensionless bootstrap representative

\[
C_\star=1
\]

and the initial directed pair

\[
(C_{r\to v_1},C_{v_1\to r})=(1,1).
\]

This is a normalization convention and not a free scalar parameter. N001
owns neither endpoint, the relation, the birth, nor the later proof that
another positive unit is equivalent.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-9}

A weighted root-child relation must enter the recurrent development with
a concrete representative. Choosing one removes an otherwise arbitrary
common scale at the bootstrap boundary. The nontrivial justification
that this choice does not add physical information must remain separate:
N001 defines the representative; M005 proves the comparison theorem.

\subsection{Construction Or Encoding}\label{construction-or-encoding-4}

Python stores no payload in \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{InitialConductanceNormalization}}.
The properties \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{value}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directed\_values}} return the
module constant \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C\_STAR=1}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(1,1)}}.

Lean uses a one-constructor inductive type. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{value}} is
definitionally \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1\ :\ Nat}}; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedValues}} duplicates
that value; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedValues\_eq\_unit\_pair}} proves the pair is
exactly \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(1,1)}} by reflexivity.

The local \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Nat}} carrier is part of the bootstrap representation
only. M005 later embeds this numeral into \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Rat}}; later effective
conductance carriers are not fixed by the present type choice.

\subsection{Boundary Case Or
Countercheck}\label{boundary-case-or-countercheck-4}

Python rejects \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{InitialConductanceNormalization(2)}},
demonstrating that \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{2}} is not an alternative state of this node.
The field audit excludes endpoint, relation, address, event and birth
payloads. The Lean type has no alternative constructor or field.

This fixedness does not imply unit-independence. Without M005, the
statement ``one is only a representative'' would be unsupported because
no alternative positive unit appears in N001's type.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-9}

N001 closes the canonical bootstrap pair \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(1,1)}} and only that
pair.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-9}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E008:\ N001\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}} supplies the
  first-relation conductances.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E010:\ N001\ -\textgreater{}\ M005}} supplies the canonical
  representative whose scale class M005 analyzes.
\end{itemize}

\section{011 · C013 --- First non-root provenance birth
v₁}\label{c013-first-non-root-provenance-birth-vux2081}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{011\ ·\ C013}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C013}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.4}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-7}

C013 is the first node that joins all exceptional-bootstrap inputs:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E007:\ C004A\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}}, the structural slot
  \(s_1\);
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E006:\ A001\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}}, the information-free seed
  token;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E008:\ N001\ -\textgreater{}\ C013}}, the unit conductance
  representative.
\end{itemize}

All three edges are \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}}. The construction precedes
T001 and C014 because those nodes certify and package its output.

\subsection{CNNA Architecture Role}\label{cnna-architecture-role-4}

C013 constructs the first nontrivial \textbf{ComplemeNt Net}. The
selected open slot becomes a newborn and is linked to the already
realized root by the first weighted directed relation. Root and newborn
are not permanent intrinsic opposites; together they form the first
realized context on which later cut-relative complements can act.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-3}

For a C004A slot

\[
s_1=(\varepsilon,0,(0))
\]

with a proof or executable check that \(|(0)|\le L\), and for the A001
token and N001 normalization, C013 constructs a first non-root birth
\(B_1\) satisfying

\[
\operatorname{root}(B_1)=\varepsilon,
\qquad
\operatorname{newborn}(B_1)=(0),
\]

\[
\operatorname{relations}(B_1)=((\varepsilon,(0)),((0),\varepsilon)),
\qquad
\operatorname{conductances}(B_1)=(1,1).
\]

The seed is an input of the constructor but not a field of the output.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-10}

The recurrent rule cannot produce the first relation from a pre-existing
response because no nontrivial weighted network exists before that
relation. C013 isolates this unavoidable exceptional initialization
instead of applying the later response-coupled law outside its domain.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-3}

Python defines the immutable record

\begin{verbatim}
FirstNonRootBirth(slot, normalization)
\end{verbatim}

and derives the root, newborn, two directed orientations and conductance
pair by projection. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{build\_first\_non\_root\_birth}} verifies the
exact predecessor types and calls
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{slot.require\_admitted\_address()}} before returning the record.

Lean defines

\begin{verbatim}
structure FirstNonRootBirth where
  slot          : FirstProvenanceSlot
  normalization : InitialConductanceNormalization
  withinCutoff  : FirstProvenanceSlot.WithinCutoff slot
\end{verbatim}

and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FirstNonRootBirth.build}} accepts the explicit seed but does
not store it. The proof of finite admission is carried in the result
type.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-3}

The current Lean source proves:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{newborn\_eq\_first\_slot}}: the newborn address is exactly the
  C004A address;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{newborn\_parent\_root}}: C003 parent reconstruction returns the
  root;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedConductances\_eq\_unit\_pair}}: the two orientations
  are \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(1,1)}}.
\end{enumerate}

The directed relation is stored in both orientations, but this symmetry
is only the bootstrap initialization. It is not a claim that all later
live directed conductances remain symmetric.

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-3}

Given the fixed C004A slot and N001 normalization, the endpoint,
relation and conductance data are definitional projections. A001 carries
no information, and the generated record contains no seed field.
Nevertheless, C013 deliberately does not own the theorem comparing two
explicit seed-indexed constructor calls; T001 owns that equality.

No separate theorem states uniqueness among all conceivable records
satisfying the displayed equations. The closed claim is the canonicity
of the provided constructor and its proved projections.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-3}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  For \(L\ge1\), C004A supplies a cutoff proof and C013 is
  constructible.
\item
  For \(L=0\), the word \((0)\) remains structurally defined, but the
  Python guard rejects it and no Lean value can be built without the
  false cutoff proposition.
\item
  C013 computes no response, geometry, event number or time.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-3}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\columnwidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\columnwidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\columnwidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\columnwidth - 6\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2500}}@{}}

\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Python
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright

predecessor typing & exact runtime \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{type}} checks & static types &

cutoff & rejecting method call & proposition argument stored in result &
same depth-one gate \\
seed & explicit argument, not stored & explicit argument, not stored &
same erasure boundary \\
endpoints and relations & properties & definitions & same C004A
projections \\
conductances & N001 property & N001 definition and theorem & same
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(1,1)}} pair \\
\end{longtable}

The representation difference for the cutoff proof is intentional and
does not create a semantic mismatch.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-3}

The focused Python tests construct the complete first relation at
\(L=1\), verify both directed orientations and the unit pair, reject
\(L=0\), and audit the dataclass fields as exactly
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{("slot",\ "normalization")}}. These tests would fail if the seed
were retained, if the child rank were shifted, if one orientation were
omitted, or if a response-dependent value entered the bootstrap.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-10}

C013 closes the exceptional first non-root birth and its initial
weighted directed relation. Its registered obligation is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CLOSED\_VERIFIED}} and is supported by the listed source
theorems.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-10}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E009:\ C013\ -\textgreater{}\ T001}} requests the explicit
  seed-neutrality theorem.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E011:\ C013\ -\textgreater{}\ C014}} supplies the concrete
  birth record.
\end{itemize}

Both edges are \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}}.

\section{012 · T001 --- Seed-neutrality theorem for first
birth}\label{t001-seed-neutrality-theorem-for-first-birth}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{012\ ·\ T001}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{T001}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.5}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-8}

T001 follows C013 through verified edge \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E009}}. A001 proves only
that the seed carrier is singleton-valued; C013 shows that the seed is
not stored. T001 is the theorem node that turns this design into an
explicit equality statement and passes the certificate to C014 through
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E012}}.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-2}

For any C004A slot \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{slot}}, seeds
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{eta\ etaPrime\ :\ GenesisSeed}}, N001 normalization
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{normalization}}, and cutoff proof \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{h}}, the current Lean
theorem states

\begin{verbatim}
firstWeightedStateFromSeed slot eta normalization h =
  firstWeightedStateFromSeed slot etaPrime normalization h
∧ directedConductances (...) = (1, 1)
\end{verbatim}

Equivalently,

\[
B(slot,\eta,N,h)=B(slot,\eta',N,h)
\quad\land\quad
\mathcal C(B(slot,\eta,N,h))=(1,1).
\]

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-2}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  one fixed structural C004A slot;
\item
  two arbitrary explicit A001 seed values;
\item
  one fixed N001 normalization;
\item
  admission of the slot into the finite cutoff.
\end{itemize}

No response, geometry, time or recurrent state is assumed.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-11}

The seed is needed to expose the exceptional bootstrap boundary, but the
model must not inherit a hidden state variable from it. Keeping T001
separate from A001 and C013 makes the no-information claim falsifiable:
a later modification that stores or uses the seed would break the
theorem rather than silently changing the meaning of the token.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-2}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{firstWeightedStateFromSeed}} is a transparent wrapper around
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FirstNonRootBirth.build}}. The build function ignores its seed
argument when creating the record. Therefore both generated states
reduce to the same term and the equality branch is proved by
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{rfl}}. The conductance branch invokes C013's
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedConductances\_eq\_unit\_pair}}.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-2}

\begin{verbatim}
C013.FirstNonRootBirth.build
  -> firstWeightedStateFromSeed
  -> definitional equality under eta / etaPrime

C013.directedConductances_eq_unit_pair
  -> second conjunct of seedNeutralityFirstBirth
\end{verbatim}

A001's \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{eqCanonical}} is available but is not used. This matters:
the current theorem follows from structural seed erasure, not merely
from rewriting all seeds to the unique constructor.

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-2}

Python's \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{first\_weighted\_state\_from\_seed}} calls the same C013
builder. The test creates two distinct \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{GenesisSeed()}} objects,
verifies distinct identity, and then checks equality of the generated
records and the pair \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(1,1)}}.

Lean quantifies over arbitrary seed values and proves the conjunction in
the mathlib-free core. The theorem is kernel-checkable with the
package's pinned Lean version and depends only on the imported C013
definitions and theorem.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-2}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Adding a \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{seed}} field to \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FirstNonRootBirth}} would
  destroy the direct \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{rfl}} proof unless equality were separately
  quotiented or rewritten.
\item
  Using the seed to choose a child address or conductance would
  invalidate the theorem even though A001 currently has one constructor;
  it would reveal a meaningless but structurally present dependency.
\item
  Removing the common cutoff hypothesis would make the two terms
  ill-typed, because C013 is not defined outside the admitted first
  slot.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-2}

The theorem body uses \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{rfl}} and an exact imported theorem. The
source is in the dependency-free core package and contains no project
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{axiom}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorry}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{admit}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{opaque}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{unsafe}} or \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{partial}} declaration; its listed source hash
is the verified current hash.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-11}

T001 proves exact state equality, not merely equality of selected
observables. The first birth leaves no seed state variable behind.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-2}

The theorem is restricted to the exceptional first birth. It says
nothing about hypothetical seed arguments in later recurrent steps, and
it does not prove M005's unit-independence statement.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-11}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E012:\ T001\ -\textgreater{}\ C014}} is \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}}
and certifies the seed-neutrality component of the packaged bootstrap
state.

\section{013 · M005 --- Conductance-unit normalization
independence}\label{m005-conductance-unit-normalization-independence}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{013\ ·\ M005}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{M005}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.6}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-9}

M005 follows N001 through verified edge \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E010}}. N001 fixes one
representative but cannot by itself establish representative
independence. M005 introduces the comparison relation and proves the
positive common-rescaling theorem before C014 uses it on the actual
stored bootstrap conductances.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-3}

Lean defines

\[
\operatorname{SameNormalizedResponse}(R,C,R',C')
\quad:\Longleftrightarrow\quad
RC'=R'C.
\]

The source proves:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  for \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{scale\ \textgreater{}\ 0}}, \[
  (R,C)\sim(scale\,R,scale\,C);
  \]
\item
  the rational lift of N001 is exactly one;
\item
  for every positive rational unit \(u\) and normalized value \(x\), \[
  (x,1)\sim(ux,u).
  \]
\end{enumerate}

Python realizes the quotient interpretation as exact
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Fraction(response)\ /\ Fraction(unit)}} with a positive-unit
guard.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-3}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  rational scalar responses and conductance units;
\item
  a positive comparison scale for an admissible unit change;
\item
  in the canonical-representative theorem, a positive alternative unit.
\end{itemize}

The algebraic cross-product relation itself is defined for all
rationals, but its interpretation as equality of quotients requires
nonzero units; the CNNA conductance convention strengthens this to
positivity.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-12}

Without M005, fixing \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C★=1}} would merely hide an unexamined
choice inside a singleton type. The present theorem introduces an
explicit family of alternative positive representatives and proves that
the dimensionless normalized datum is unchanged. This makes the claim
``no third physical input'' falsifiable.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-3}

The common-rescaling theorem avoids division:

\[
R(\lambda C)=(R\lambda)C=(\lambda R)C.
\]

The canonical-representative theorem first rewrites the N001 rational
lift to one and then uses commutativity and the right-unit law. Exact
rational arithmetic is used throughout.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-3}

\begin{verbatim}
InitialConductanceNormalization.value
  -> n001ConductanceUnit
  -> n001ConductanceUnit_eq_one

SameNormalizedResponse
  + Rat.mul_assoc
  + Rat.mul_comm
  -> commonPositiveRescalingPreservesNormalizedResponse

n001ConductanceUnit_eq_one
  + Rat.mul_comm
  + Rat.mul_one
  -> n001CanonicalRepresentativeForPositiveUnit
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-3}

Python's \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{normalized\_response}} rejects a nonpositive unit and
computes an exact \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Fraction}}.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{common\_positive\_rescaling\_preserves\_normalized\_response}}
rejects a nonpositive scale and compares the pre- and post-rescaling
quotients exactly. The test includes positive integer and
proper-fraction scales, positive and negative response values, and
verifies that N001 normalization returns the exact response coordinate.

Lean imports only the core rational lemmas and N001. The theorem is
division-free, so no inverse or regularization enters the proof.
Positivity is retained as the semantic admissibility condition even
though the polynomial identity does not consume it computationally.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-3}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(R,C)\ -\textgreater{}\ (lambda\ R,C)}} changes the quotient
  unless \(\lambda=1\) or \(R=0\).
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(R,C)\ -\textgreater{}\ (R,lambda\ C)}} likewise changes it in
  general.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{lambda=0}} satisfies the bare cross-product equation but
  destroys the unit and is rejected by Python and by the positive-scale
  hypothesis.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{lambda\textless{}0}} preserves an algebraic ratio but leaves
  the positive-conductance domain.
\item
  Edge-dependent or orientation-dependent rescalings are not covered by
  the theorem.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-3}

The Lean source is in the mathlib-free core package, uses transparent
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Rat}} definitions and elementary rational lemmas, and contains no
project axiom or admitted proof.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-12}

N001's value one is a canonical representative of a positive rational
scaling class for the scalar normalized response. The comparison
variable is not a CNNA input and is not stored in the state.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-3}

M005 does not prove homogeneity of the entire matrix-valued Schur/DtN
construction and does not establish local gauge symmetry. Its exact
downstream role is narrower: it supplies the fixed positive unit
representative \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C★=1}} consumed by M003 after the C015 identity
transform.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-12}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E013:\ M005\ -\textgreater{}\ C014}} is
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and certifies the actual bootstrap
  conductance pair.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E068:\ M005\ -\textgreater{}\ M003}} is
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and supplies the fixed unit representative
  used by the canonical steering scalar.
\end{itemize}

\section{014 · C014 --- Bootstrap state
X₁}\label{c014-bootstrap-state-xux2081}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{014\ ·\ C014}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C014}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.2.7}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-10}

C014 is the terminal construction of the exceptional-bootstrap group. It
has three verified hard predecessors:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E011:\ C013\ -\textgreater{}\ C014}}, the concrete first birth;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E012:\ T001\ -\textgreater{}\ C014}}, seed-neutrality;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E013:\ M005\ -\textgreater{}\ C014}}, unit-representative
  independence.
\end{itemize}

Its verified outgoing edge \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E014:\ C014\ -\textgreater{}\ C005}}
supplies the base case for recurrent states.

\subsection{CNNA Architecture Role}\label{cnna-architecture-role-5}

C014 is the first response-capable CNNA state. It packages the weighted
root/newborn net from which later nodes can form retained/complement
cuts, eliminate interiors, and couple effective response back into
growth. C014 is only the base carrier; it does not yet perform those
operations.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-4}

The state \(X_1\) contains

\[
V_1=\{\varepsilon,(0)\},
\quad
\mathcal R_1=((\varepsilon,(0)),((0),\varepsilon)),
\quad
\mathcal C_1=(1,1).
\]

The implementation represents this information minimally by storing the
C013 birth once and deriving the displayed components by projection. The
set notation is a mathematical view, not a claim that the runtime object
contains a redundant set field.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-13}

C013 creates the exceptional relation, but the recurrent layer needs a
named state object that packages the relation and carries the two
no-hidden-input certificates. C014 marks the exact transition from
initialization to the domain on which later response constructions can
act.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-4}

Python defines

\begin{verbatim}
BootstrapState(birth : FirstNonRootBirth)
\end{verbatim}

with projections \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{root}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{newborn}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directed\_relations}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directed\_conductances}}.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{build\_bootstrap\_state}} is the one-field constructor.

Lean defines the same one-field structure and constructor. It then
provides:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  root and newborn projections;
\item
  the inherited unit-pair theorem;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{fromSeed\_seedNeutral}}, obtained by applying
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{congrArg\ build}} to T001;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedConductancesRat}}, the rational lift of the actual
  stored pair;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedConductancesRat\_eq\_n001\_pair}};
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{conductanceUnit\_isRepresentativeOnly}}, which applies M005 to
  each orientation.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-4}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  the carrier view contains exactly root and first newborn;
\item
  the relation contains both provenance-parent orientations;
\item
  both stored conductances are one;
\item
  no seed field is present;
\item
  no variable unit field is present;
\item
  the comparison scale used by M005 produces no alternative
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{BootstrapState}} value.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-4}

For each C013 birth there is exactly the transparent one-field wrapper
produced by \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{build}}. C014 does not prove uniqueness of C013
itself; it inherits C013's data. The T001 theorem proves independence
from explicit seed arguments, and M005 proves representative invariance
of the normalized conductance coordinate. Together these exclude the two
intended hidden bootstrap choices.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-4}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(L=0\): C013 cannot be constructed, hence neither can C014.
\item
  \(L\ge1\): C014 is available once the C013 birth is supplied.
\item
  The phrase ``response-capable'' means a nontrivial weighted relation
  exists. No Schur complement, DtN response, steering scalar, geometry,
  event time or recurrent update is evaluated here.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-4}

Python stores only the birth and exposes executable projections. Lean
stores the same birth and adds proof theorems around it. These theorems
do not enlarge the runtime payload. The rational lift in Lean is a
proof-side view of the natural-number pair used for M005; Python's C014
module does not duplicate that proof because the exact scalar theorem is
tested in M005's module.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-4}

The Python test constructs the complete chain from grammar through C014,
checks root, newborn, both relations and both conductances, and audits
the dataclass fields as exactly \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{("birth",)}}. The constructor's
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\_\_post\_init\_\_}} rejects a value of any other exact type.
These checks exclude duplicated state components and hidden certificate
variables.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-13}

C014 closes the first response-capable weighted provenance state and the
exceptional initialization section. The D6 registry synchronization
marks its obsolete blocked-obligation row as \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CLOSED\_VERIFIED}};
no mathematical source is changed.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-13}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E014:\ C014\ -\textgreater{}\ C005}} is
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and supplies the recurrent base case.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E077:\ C014\ -\textgreater{}\ C019}} and
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E153:\ C014\ -\textgreater{}\ P005}} remain
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{PLANNED\_BLOCKED}}; they require the later recurrent iteration
  and proof closure.
\end{itemize}

\section{015 · C005 --- Response-capable state schema X\_n, n
\textgreater= 1}\label{c005-response-capable-state-schema-x_n-n-1}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{015\ ·\ C005}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-11}

C005 begins the recurrent layer after C014. It is the domain of every
later pre-birth measurement and update step.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-5}

A state \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{X\_n}} consists of a C003 grammar, its C018 schedule, a
nonempty list \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bornNonRoot}}, and a finite list of positive
directed conductances. The mathematical birth count is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{n\ =\ bornNonRoot.length}}, hence \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{n\ \textgreater{}=\ 1}}.
The born non-root list is exactly the first \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{n}} schedule
children. The carrier is the root together with this prefix.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-14}

C014 supplies only the exceptional base state. Recurrent growth needs a
stable state type independent of the next-slot, cut, response, steering,
and update constructions.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-5}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{DirectedConductance}} stores a source address, a distinct target
address, and a strictly positive exact rational value.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCapableState}} stores the grammar, schedule, born prefix,
and conductance list. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{fromBootstrap}} transports the C014
root/newborn pair and its two directed unit values into the recurrent
representation.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-5}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  grammar and schedule share the same branching parameter and cutoff;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bornNonRoot}} is nonempty, duplicate-free, cutoff-admissible,
  and equal to the initial C018 prefix;
\item
  every conductance has born endpoints, positive value, and distinct
  endpoints;
\item
  ordered conductance pairs are unique;
\item
  every born non-root address has both positive parent orientations;
\item
  additional directed edges between already-born vertices are permitted.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-5}

C005 does not claim a unique state for fixed \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{n}}; live
conductance values may differ. It claims a unique schema and a canonical
embedding of C014. The theorem \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{fromBootstrap\_n}} fixes the base
image at \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{n=1}}.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-5}

The schema excludes \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{n=0}}; that regime is owned by C001/C002
before the first non-root birth. Self-loops, nonpositive values, unborn
endpoints, duplicate ordered pairs, and non-prefix birth lists are
rejected. Saturation is allowed as a state condition but yields no C004
successor.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-5}

Python enforces the invariants in immutable dataclasses. Lean stores the
same obligations as fields of \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCapableState}} and proves
the C014 transport facts. Lean does not need to mirror Python exception
classes; the semantic agreement is the accepted-state predicate.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-5}

Removing the initial-prefix condition would make positional C004
selection unsound. Removing either parent orientation would destroy the
guaranteed bidirectional provenance backbone. Forbidding additional
born-born edges would impose an unsupported tree-only live network.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-14}

C005 is a verified response-capable recurrent state domain with exact
rational directed conductances and a canonical C014 base inhabitant.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-14}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E015}} to C004: exposes the next open slot;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E016}} to M001: supplies the born carrier;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E022}} to C007: supplies the current directed network;
\item
  later codomain/update gates remain open.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Open-provenance role: Sufficient finite provenance
state}\label{open-provenance-role-sufficient-finite-provenance-state}

C005 is the current finite candidate for a provenance-sufficient state:
it carries the born prefix and response data needed by the next CNNA
update. Whether an arbitrary empirical reduced state admits such a
completion is outside the present theorem scope.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{016 · C004 --- Next open provenance slot
s\_\{n+1\}}\label{c004-next-open-provenance-slot-s_n1}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{016\ ·\ C004}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.2}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-12}

C004 consumes a recurrent C005 state and the already fixed C018 order.
It is the unique structural selector used by M001 and later birth nodes.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-6}

For an unsaturated state \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{X}}, an admissible open address is a
non-root C003 address within cutoff that is not born.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsNextOpenAddress\ X\ a}} means that \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{a}} is admissible and
no admissible open address precedes it under C018 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{BirthBefore}}.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{NextOpenSlot\ X}} packages this child with its uniquely
reconstructed parent and final rank.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-15}

The recurrent layer needs the next structural provenance location before
it can define a local cut or measure a response. This selector must use
no response value, geometry, or birth law.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-6}

Python returns \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{state.schedule.slots{[}state.birth\_count{]}}}
after checking unsaturation and verifies it through
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{is\_next\_open\_provenance\_slot}}. Lean finitely enumerates the
C003 carrier, filters open candidates, and chooses the least candidate
constructively under C018.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-6}

The child is non-root, cutoff-admissible, not born, and least among all
open addresses. Every admissible predecessor is born. The reconstructed
parent is born and the child equals \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{parent.snoc(rank)}}.

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-6}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{exists\_of\_unsaturated}} proves existence;
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{child\_unique}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{unique}} prove uniqueness.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{parent\_rank\_unique}} proves that the child determines exactly
one parent/rank pair.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-6}

For a saturated finite approximant, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{no\_next\_of\_saturated}}
proves that no successor exists. No sentinel or out-of-cutoff child is
introduced. At \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{L=0}}, every recurrent state premise is already
unavailable because C014 cannot be formed.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-6}

Python is positional because C005 proves the born list is the exact
schedule prefix. Lean is extensional and proves least-open uniqueness.
Their semantic lock is the independent Python predicate matching the
Lean relation.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-6}

An arbitrary un-born child would not be canonical. A second schedule
would duplicate C018. A sentinel would falsely add a provenance address
beyond the finite carrier. Omitting parent-bornness would invalidate the
M001 causal prefix cut.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-15}

C004 provides exactly one next open provenance slot for every
unsaturated C005 state and none for a saturated state.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-15}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E017}} to M001 localizes the cut;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E026}} to M004 is \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and supplies the
  unique next provenance slot used by the birth instruction;
\item
  C004 also supplies the verified dynamic least-open content that P002
  had attempted to own too early.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Open-provenance role: Open slot as state-relative
incompleteness}\label{open-provenance-role-open-slot-as-state-relative-incompleteness}

C004 makes openness relative to a current born prefix: the next slot is
absent from the present state but admissible in the fixed provenance
grammar. This is the finite CNNA specialization of openness relative to
an incomplete state description.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{017 · C006 --- Birth-local Schur/DtN
primitive}\label{c006-birth-local-schurdtn-primitive}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{017\ ·\ C006}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.3}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-13}

C006 is introduced at the first use of block elimination, before any
state-dependent matrix assembly. It is a generic exact partial operator
used by M002, C007, and P001.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-7}

For ordered blocks \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_BB}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_BI}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_IB}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_II}} with nonempty boundary, an interior solve \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{X}}
satisfies \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_II\ X\ =\ K\_IB}}. The exact domain is existence of
exactly one encoded solve. On that domain the response value is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_BB\ -\ K\_BI\ X}}.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-16}

The cut selector and the network realization must not be conflated with
the algebraic elimination rule. C006 isolates the reusable mathematical
primitive and makes partiality explicit.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-7}

Python uses exact \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Fraction}} matrices and Gauss-Jordan
elimination without tolerance. Lean defines positive-denominator raw
fractions, cross-multiplication value equality, matrix
multiplication/subtraction, solve and response predicates, and the
zero-interior witness.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-7}

Boundary coordinates precede interior coordinates. Matrix dimensions are
type- or constructor-checked. No transpose, symmetrization,
pseudoinverse, regularization, threshold, or condition-number rule is
admitted.

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-7}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{response\_exists\_of\_admissible}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{response\_unique\_of\_admissible}} prove existence and
value-level uniqueness. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{response\_of\_sameValue}} proves
independence of raw fraction representation. Structural
numerator/denominator equality is intentionally not required.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-7}

Boundary size must be positive. Interior size may be zero; then the
unique empty solve exists and the response is \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_BB}}. A singular
nonempty \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_II}} is outside the domain rather than regularized
numerically.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-7}

Python returns normalized fractions. Lean's core relation compares raw
positive-denominator encodings by cross multiplication. P001 later
proves the exact semantic bridge to ordinary rational matrix arithmetic;
that later theorem is not silently imported into C006.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-7}

Making the operator total with a pseudoinverse would change the model.
Comparing raw encodings structurally would make the response depend on
normalization. Permuting coordinates inside C006 would violate ownership
of the M001 order.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-16}

C006 is a verified exact, partial, representative-independent Schur/DtN
response primitive.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-16}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E019}} to C007 supplies elimination;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E135}} to P001 supplies the native exact interface;
\item
  later general-cut reuse remains open.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Open-provenance role: Exact complement
elimination}\label{open-provenance-role-exact-complement-elimination}

C006 realizes one specific open-provenance reduction: exact directed
Schur/DtN elimination by a unique interior solve. It is not a partial
trace, marginalization, or quantum instrument.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{018 · M001 --- Canonical birth-local measurement cut
C\_n(s\_\{n+1\})}\label{m001-canonical-birth-local-measurement-cut-c_ns_n1}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{018\ ·\ M001}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.4}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-14}

M001 follows C005 and C004. It owns the state- and slot-dependent
ordered cut and owns proof gate P003 internally.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-4}

For \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{X\_n}} and next slot \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(p,rho,c)}}, the boundary is the
canonical-carrier subsequence consisting of the root-to-parent prefix
chain of \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{p}} together with born same-parent siblings of ranks
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\textless{}\ rho}}. The interior is the complementary
canonical-carrier subsequence. The unborn child \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{c}} is in neither
list.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-4}

A valid C005 state, a valid C004 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{NextOpenSlot}}, the inherited
C003 grammar, and the inherited C018 order. No response, conductance
value, geometry, or downstream steering hypothesis is used.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-17}

The C006 operator requires explicit ordered boundary/interior
coordinates. Birth locality must therefore be derived from provenance
before numerical block entries are assembled.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-4}

Construct one canonical carrier list, define a decidable birth-local
port predicate, and obtain boundary/interior by complementary filtering.
Prove that every selected causal predecessor and older sibling is born,
then derive partition properties from the common carrier filter.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-4}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{prefixChainAux\_mem\_prefix}} -\textgreater{}
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{causalPredecessorPort\_born}};
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{earlier\_admissible\_is\_born}} from C004 -\textgreater{}
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{olderSiblingPort\_born}}; these yield
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{birthLocalPort\_born}}. Filtering yields \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{boundary}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{interior}}; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalCut\_isCanonical}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{unique}} close canonicity. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{boundary\_node\_born}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{interior\_node\_born}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{boundary\_interior\_disjoint}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{carrier\_covered}}, and the child-exclusion theorems close P003.

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-4}

Python constructs immutable filtered tuples and performs runtime
partition assertions. Lean defines the same predicates over the
canonical carrier and proves the complete owner certificate in
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{S04\_M001\_CanonicalBirthLocalMeasurementCutCnSnplus1.lean}}.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-4}

Including the unborn child would turn a pre-birth measurement into a
postulated-node measurement. Sorting the two blocks anew would add a
second ordering convention. Using weights to select ports would make
locality response-dependent. Omitting complementary coverage would leave
the C007 state matrix under-specified.

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-4}

The module is in the mathlib-free core and contains no project-local
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{axiom}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorry}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{admit}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{opaque}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{unsafe}}, or \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{partial}} declaration. The current 26-job
core build evidence certifies the listed source.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-17}

M001 and its P003 owner gate are closed: the cut is unique, born-only,
disjoint, exhaustive, order-preserving by filtering, and strictly
pre-birth.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-4}

M001 supplies no numerical block entries and proves no interior
admissibility. P007 remains a later general-cut generalization and does
not keep M001 yellow.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-17}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E018}} to M002 supplies cut dimensions;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E020}} to C007 supplies coordinate order;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E146}} records P003 certification of this owner.
\end{itemize}

\section{019 · P003 --- Birth-cut canonicity and partition
well-formedness}\label{p003-birth-cut-canonicity-and-partition-well-formedness}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{019\ ·\ P003}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.4.1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-15}

P003 is the explicit proof-certification expansion of M001. It
introduces no new cut and no parallel derivation branch.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-5}

For every valid C005 state and C004 successor, the M001 boundary and
interior are born-only, disjoint, exhaustive on the current born
carrier, preserve inherited carrier order, exclude the unborn child, and
are uniquely determined by the canonical selector.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-5}

Exactly the hypotheses already carried by M001: C005 state invariants
and the C004 next-slot certificate. C018 order is inherited
transitively. No P002 dependency is required by the proof term.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-18}

M001's construction is scientifically usable by C007 only after its
partition and pre-birth properties are independently visible as one
falsifiable proof gate.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-5}

Reuse the owner definitions. Bornness is proved for the port predicate;
complementary filters yield disjointness and coverage; filter order is
inherited definitionally; canonical-cut equality yields uniqueness; C004
non-bornness yields child exclusion.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-5}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalCarrier}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{boundary}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{interior}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsCanonicalCut}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalCut\_isCanonical}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{unique}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{boundary\_node\_born}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{interior\_node\_born}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{boundary\_interior\_disjoint}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{carrier\_covered}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{child\_not\_in\_boundary}}, and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{child\_not\_in\_interior}}.

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-5}

The complete certificate is intentionally internal to the M001 Lean
module. P003's code register therefore contains supporting-evidence
anchors into that owner module and its independent Python
construction/test, rather than a duplicate P003 source file.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-5}

A boundary containing an un-born address violates the C005 domain.
Non-disjoint blocks duplicate matrix coordinates. Non-exhaustive blocks
omit live conductances. Reordering a filter result changes the matrix
basis. Including the next child imports nonexistent state. Without
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsCanonicalCut}}, multiple witnesses could satisfy a weaker
partition predicate.

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-5}

The supporting theorems are in the mathlib-free core and contain no
project-local axiom or admitted proof. No new proof source is introduced
in D7.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-18}

P003 is closed by exact owner-internal theorems. Its certification edge
to M001 is active and verified.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-5}

General-cut proof P007 remains future work.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-18}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E146}} certifies M001. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E165}} later allows P007 to reuse
this birth-local special case but remains blocked.

\section{020 · M002 --- Birth-cut interior-domain
theorem}\label{m002-birth-cut-interior-domain-theorem}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{020\ ·\ M002}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.5}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-16}

M002 connects the M001 cut to the partial C006 primitive before C007
supplies state-dependent numerical entries.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-6}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{BirthCutBlocks\ next}} has exactly
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\textbar{}B\_n\textbar{}}} boundary and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\textbar{}I\_n\textbar{}}} interior coordinates.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{InExactDomain\ next\ blocks}} is definitionally C006
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsInteriorAdmissible\ blocks}}. On this domain a C006 response
exists and is unique in exact fraction value.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-6}

A valid M001 next-slot cut and explicitly supplied C006 blocks of the
cut-induced dimensions. Nonempty-interior admissibility is a hypothesis,
not a consequence of dimensions alone.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-19}

The original obligation allowed either a universal invertibility theorem
or an exact domain statement. M001 contains no numerical entries, so
only the exact-domain branch is derivable at this point.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-6}

Derive nonempty boundary from the root causal port. Encode dimension
agreement in the type of \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{BirthCutBlocks}}. Reuse C006
admissibility for response existence/uniqueness. Prove zero-interior
membership directly from C006.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-6}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{root\_is\_birthLocalPort}} -\textgreater{}
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalBoundary\_nonempty}}; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{mkBirthCutBlocks}};
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{InExactDomain}}; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{inExactDomain\_iff\_c006\_admissible}};
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{exactDomain\_response\_exists}};
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{exactDomain\_response\_unique}};
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{zeroInterior\_inExactDomain}}.

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-6}

Python validates dimensions, calls the exact C006 domain predicate, and
constructs an identity-versus-zero same-cut contrast witness. Lean
encodes dimensions in types and proves the exact handoff without
duplicating the executable contrast.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-6}

For any nonempty interior, identity \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_II}} and zero
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K\_II}} have the same dimensions but different admissibility.
Therefore dimensions, provenance locality, boundary nonemptiness,
positivity of stored conductances, or notation alone cannot justify a
total Schur complement.

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-6}

The module belongs to the mathlib-free core and introduces no
project-local admitted theorem. Its source is unchanged by D7.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-19}

M002 closes the domain obligation exactly: zero interior is
unconditional; every other case requires exact unique solvability.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-6}

M002 does not prove that every reachable C007 realization lies in the
domain. Generic first-hit reachability and linear well-posedness are
treated later in P001; exact canonical-cut instantiation remains a
separate proof target there.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-19}

\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E021}} certifies C007's domain;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E136}} supplies the exact interior-domain interface to P001.
\end{itemize}

\section{021 · C007 --- Inter-birth directed response
R\_n(s\_\{n+1\})}\label{c007-inter-birth-directed-response-r_ns_n1}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{021\ ·\ C007}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.6}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-17}

C007 is the first state-dependent response node. It combines C005,
C004/M001, M002, and C006 after all structural and domain ownership has
been fixed.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-8}

Order the entire born carrier as M001 boundary followed by interior.
From every stored directed conductance define the source/out-degree
matrix \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K{[}u,u{]}=sum\_v\ c(u,v)}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{K{[}u,v{]}=-c(u,v)}} for \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{u\ !=\ v}}. On M002 domain
membership, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{R\_n(s\_\{n+1\})}} is the unique C006 Schur/DtN
response.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-20}

The later steering law needs a measured property of the existing network
before the next birth. This response must use the current directed
weights without symmetrization or a response-independent bias.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-8}

Python assembles an exact \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Fraction}} matrix, slices the four
blocks in M001 order, validates M002, and evaluates C006. Lean
constructs exact-fraction outgoing/ordered-pair sums, defines raw
blocks, and requires a \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{StateDirectedBlockRealization}} proving
that canonical rational inputs represent those exact values.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-8}

Rows are sources and columns targets. Off-diagonal entries are negative
ordered-pair conductances; the diagonal is total outgoing conductance.
All current born vertices occur exactly once. The unborn child occurs
nowhere. No external port, grounded load, geometry, averaging,
transpose, or regularization is added.

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-8}

M001 fixes coordinates, the directed conductance list fixes exact
entries, M002 fixes the domain, and C006 proves response existence and
value-level uniqueness. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{response\_of\_sameValue}} makes
output-representative independence explicit.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-8}

Zero interior reduces to the boundary block. Singular nonempty interior
is outside the domain. Saturated states have no C004 slot and therefore
no C007 pre-birth response indexed by \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{s\_\{n+1\}}}.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-8}

Python normalizes fractions; Lean separates exact values from canonical
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Rat}} input representatives through \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{MatrixRepresents}}.
The P001 semantic bridge later identifies these with ordinary rational
matrix arithmetic. Both layers preserve the same M001 coordinate order
and source/out-degree sign convention.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-8}

Symmetrizing would erase directional data. Reversing row/column
ownership would transpose the model. Adding the unborn child would make
the measurement anticipatory. A tolerance-based inverse would alter
M002. Omitting additional born-born edges would fail to represent the
live C005 state.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-20}

C007 is a verified exact directed pre-birth response on the stated M002
domain.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-20}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E024}} to M003 is \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ACTIVE\_VERIFIED}} and supplies the
  exact response consumed by the canonical steering functional;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E035}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E037}}, and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E038}} feed null/robustness
  controls;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E137}} supplies the directed block operator to P001.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Open-provenance role: Effective response after complement
elimination}\label{open-provenance-role-effective-response-after-complement-elimination}
