the finite directed-linear effective dynamics associated with the M001
cut, before scalar steering or event creation.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{022 · O001 --- IST response-independent directed-bias
obstruction}\label{o001-ist-response-independent-directed-bias-obstruction}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{022\ ·\ O001}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{O001}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.7}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-18}

O001 is introduced after the exact pre-birth response C007 and before
the active steering and birth-law handoff. Its role is to prevent the
retained legacy implementation from reintroducing numerical channels
that are independent of the response-derived scalar.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-7}

Let

\begin{verbatim}
chi = (rank, forward, backward, node_load_scalar, nonlinear_mode,
       backreaction_scale, additive_baseline, geometric_attenuation)
\end{verbatim}

be the explicit Boolean presence record. A candidate
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(state,\ slot,\ response,\ steering,\ chi)}} is admissible
exactly when \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{chi}} equals the all-false record. The accepted
output preserves \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{state}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{slot}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{response}}, and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{steering}} exactly and contains no bias record.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-7}

The obstruction is source-bound to the retained legacy growth path and
to the eight represented mechanism classes. It assumes neither that
these names exhaust every imaginable response-independent mechanism nor
that no alternative mathematical growth law could contain additional
terms.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-21}

Earlier auditing covered only rank and forward/backward asymmetry. A
later completeness review of the same executable path exposed five
further classes: node-load scalars, nonlinear modes, fixed backreaction
scales, additive baselines, and geometric attenuation. The admission
gate must therefore cover all eight classes before M004 can be regarded
as bias-free.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-7}

Python supplies an executable AST audit and a runtime admission guard.
Lean supplies a generic eight-field record, exact equality with the
all-false witness, field-preservation theorems, and one contradiction
theorem per active field. The two layers agree on field names, the
complete witness, and the four-field admitted output.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-7}

\begin{verbatim}
IndependentDirectedBiasPresence
  -> noIndependentDirectedBias
  -> IsRemoved / IsAdmissible
  -> acceptBiasFree
  -> accepted_preserves_state/slot/response/steering

one active field
  -> *_blocks_acceptance

legacyResponseIndependentChannels
  -> legacy_channels_not_removed
  -> legacy_candidate_not_admissible
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-7}

The Python AST traversal records executable names and assignments in six
bound growth functions. Comments and docstrings are not AST symbol uses.
Synthetic detection of additive baselines and geometric attenuation is
restricted to executable arithmetic patterns. The Lean module contains
no Mathlib import and was included in the user-reported prefix-free
26-job Core build.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-7}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Activating any one of the eight fields must reject the candidate.
\item
  The incomplete three-channel witness must still reject, but it is not
  treated as complete.
\item
  The full eight-channel witness must reject.
\item
  A source containing the forbidden words only in comments or docstrings
  must not trigger the AST audit.
\item
  Acceptance must preserve object identity for all four admitted Python
  fields and must erase the bias record.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-7}

O001 belongs to the Mathlib-free Core. The current evidence is a
successful Lean typecheck/root build plus a static policy scan. No
stronger claim about a separate \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\#print\ axioms}} transcript for
O001 is made.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-21}

The represented legacy mechanisms are excluded from the active M004
dependency tuple. O001 is a closed and falsifiable implementation
obstruction.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-7}

This is not a universal mathematical no-go theorem and does not prove
M003 positivity. The AST audit is exact for the bound retained source
and the declared mechanism classes only.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-21}

O001 hands M004 exactly \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(X\_n,\ s\_\{n+1\},\ R\_n,\ Sigma\_b)}}
and certifies that the canonical candidate uses the all-false presence
record.

\section{023 · C015 --- Active linear steering convention
phi(x)=x}\label{c015-active-linear-steering-convention-phixx}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{023\ ·\ C015}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C015}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.8}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D0}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-19}

C015 is placed after the obstruction gate and before M003. It fixes how
an already selected exact response scalar is transformed on the active
path.

\subsection{Definition Or Statement}\label{definition-or-statement-5}

For every scalar type \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{S}},

\begin{verbatim}
phi : S -> S
phi(x) = x
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-22}

M003 needs one explicit active-path transform, while robustness controls
must remain separate and falsifiable. The identity convention introduces
no new model parameter.

\subsection{Construction Or Encoding}\label{construction-or-encoding-5}

Python returns its single positional-only argument unchanged. Lean
defines \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{phi\ x\ :=\ x}} and proves \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{phi\_eq\_input}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{phi\_eq\_identity}} by reflexivity.

\subsection{Boundary Case Or
Countercheck}\label{boundary-case-or-countercheck-5}

The API must preserve the exact input object and expose no
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{mode}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{scale}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{slope}}, or \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{coefficient}}.
Names associated with logarithmic, saturated, symmetric, or null
controls must not appear in the public export set.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-22}

C015 contributes no numerical operation beyond identity and no branch
selection.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-22}

The unchanged exact scalar is consumed by M003 after N001/M005 unit
normalization.

\section{024 · M003 --- Canonical response-steering functional
Sigma\_b{[}R\_n,s{]}}\label{m003-canonical-response-steering-functional-sigma_br_ns}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{024\ ·\ M003}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{M003}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.9}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-20}

M003 receives the exact C007 response in M001 boundary order and
supplies the unique exact scalar and proved positive domain consumed by
M004.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-8}

For \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{p\ =\ parentAddress(next)}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sigma(next,lambda)}} is
the address-filtered sum of the \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{lambda{[}i,i{]}}} terms whose
boundary address equals \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{p}}. With \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C\_star\ =\ 1}} and the
C015 identity transform, no further normalization changes the value.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalM003Closure\ realization}} states that the canonical
realization lies in \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{InPositiveSteeringDomain}}, has a
response-steering pair, and every response-steering pair has
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{PositiveSteering}}.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-8}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{next}} is the canonical C004 slot of a C005 response-capable
  state.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{realization}} is the actual C005/M001/C006/C007 state-directed
  block realization.
\item
  The P001 reusable directed-cut hypotheses are derived for that
  realization; no parent coordinate or positivity witness is a public
  input.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-23}

C007 returns a boundary response matrix while M004 requires one
provenance-selected scalar. The closure must discharge the distinguished
coordinate internally so downstream code cannot choose a different port.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-8}

Core establishes address membership, exact aggregation, uniqueness, and
representative invariance. P001 establishes response well-posedness and
strict distinguished-port positivity. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalM003Closure}}
obtains the parent coordinate from
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{distinguishedParentIndex\_exists}}, constructs the internal
witness, and packages the resulting domain, existence, and universal
positivity statements.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-8}

\begin{verbatim}
parent_mem_boundary -> sigma -> responseSteeringPair_exists
P001 canonical cut closure -> canonicalInPositiveSteeringDomain
canonicalInPositiveSteeringDomain -> canonicalResponseSteeringPair_positive
internal distinguishedParentIndex_exists -> canonicalM003Closure
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-8}

The mathlib-free Core defines the scalar and predicates. The proof
module \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{S01\_CanonicalM003Closure.lean}} imports only the verified
P001 facade and exports a public theorem whose only explicit data
argument is the canonical realization.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-8}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  A missing or duplicated parent address cannot be repaired by a
  selected coordinate.
\item
  Zero is retained as an exact negative control but is not in the active
  positive domain.
\item
  Equivalent fraction or matrix representatives must not alter the
  scalar.
\item
  Rank, sibling number, depth, clipping, baseline, or hidden mode
  parameters are absent.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-8}

P001 remains bound to its verified 142-declaration profile. All four
M003 closure declarations are kernel-compiled and axiom-audited: two use
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{propext}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Quot.sound}}, and two additionally use
transitive \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Classical.choice}}. No project-local axiom or
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorry}} is admitted.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-23}

M003 has a closed end-to-end interface without an external parent index:
canonical response-domain inhabitance, response-steering existence, and
universal strict positivity are packaged in
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalM003Closure}}.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-8}

The result is finite and rational. Transitive \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{propext}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Classical.choice}}, and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Quot.sound}} remain within the
explicitly admitted Lean/mathlib trust boundary; their elimination is
not claimed.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-23}

M004 consumes \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalM003Closure}} directly. It does not
reconstruct the Schur/DtN proof and does not receive positivity as a
caller-supplied assumption.

\subsection{Open-provenance role: Response-to-event
specialization}\label{open-provenance-role-response-to-event-specialization}

M003 converts the effective boundary response into the unique positive
scalar used by the next event. This is the response-coupling step of the
current deterministic specialization, not a universal law for all open
systems.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{025 · P001 --- Reusable directed Schur/DtN/Kron channel
closure}\label{p001-reusable-directed-schurdtnkron-channel-closure}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{025\ ·\ P001}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{P001}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.9.1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-21}

P001 is the sole proof owner for the reusable directed Schur/DtN/Kron
closure used by C006, C007, M003, and M004. The Core package remains
mathlib-free. The proof package may import pinned mathlib and may depend
on Core; the reverse dependency is forbidden.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-9}

For finite boundary and interior index types, ordered rational blocks
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{kBB}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{kBI}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{kIB}}, and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{kII}}, and a
distinguished boundary coordinate, P001 proves the unregularized closure
from four explicit hypotheses:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  all off-diagonal entries of the full block operator are nonpositive;
\item
  every full row sum is exactly zero;
\item
  every interior coordinate reaches the boundary along positive arcs;
\item
  the distinguished boundary coordinate reaches a different boundary
  coordinate along a positive path.
\end{itemize}

The resulting public contract includes exact semantic agreement,
interior-solve existence and uniqueness, C006 admissibility, response
existence, response-witness independence, directed-Laplacian response
structure, strict distinguished-port positivity, the canonical M003
scalar handoff, M003/M004 supporting theorems, and independent reuse on
a second cut family.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-9}

The theorem uses the original ordered blocks. It assumes no symmetry,
reversibility, inverse, pseudoinverse, regularization, additional
grounding vertex, or separately postulated strong connectivity. For the
canonical birth-local cut, the four generic hypotheses are derived from
C005, M001, M002, and C007. For the independent cut, they are proved
directly from two strictly positive rational edge weights.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-24}

C006 defines a directed Schur/DtN response at the Core level, while M003
requires a strict positive parent-port response. Without one explicit
proof owner, analytic invertibility, sign conventions, representative
independence, and the canonical connectivity argument could be
duplicated or silently strengthened. P001 centralizes those obligations
and makes reuse falsifiable.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-9}

\subsubsection{Exact semantic bridge}\label{exact-semantic-bridge}

Core \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ExactFraction}} values are mapped to rational values, and
Core matrix addition, multiplication, subtraction, interior solve,
harmonic sign, and response construction are proved entrywise equivalent
to transparent rational operations. Rectangular multiplication is stated
as the explicit row-by-column finite sum.

\subsubsection{Directed maximum principle and finite
solve}\label{directed-maximum-principle-and-finite-solve}

A zero-boundary harmonic function satisfies a maximum-defect sum
identity. Every defect is nonnegative; zero total defect forces equality
across each positive arc. Positive reachability propagates an interior
maximum to the boundary, forcing the zero-boundary solution to vanish.
The resulting interior linear map is injective; finite equal dimension
gives surjectivity and hence existence and uniqueness of the solve.

\subsubsection{Response structure and strict
positivity}\label{response-structure-and-strict-positivity}

Harmonic boundary basis functions lie between zero and one. This yields
nonpositive off-diagonal response entries and exact row conservation.
The distinguished diagonal is nonnegative. If it were zero, the value
one would propagate along the distinguished positive path to a different
boundary port whose Dirichlet value is zero, a contradiction.

\subsubsection{Canonical cut derivation}\label{canonical-cut-derivation}

M001 coordinates are proved duplicate-free and complete. C007 entries
are rewritten as outgoing-degree indicators minus ordered-pair
conductance sums. Stored positive conductances induce positive arcs.
Interior paths follow the provenance parent relation and decrease depth
strictly until the boundary is reached. The distinguished parent reaches
another boundary port through the stored bidirectional backbone.

\subsubsection{M003/M004 supporting
interface}\label{m003m004-supporting-interface}

The unique canonical parent coordinate reduces M003's address-filtered
parent aggregate to the distinguished response diagonal. The P001 M004
predicate hides only proposition-valued positivity evidence;
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Subsingleton.elim}} aligns such proofs before invoking the
existing Core uniqueness theorem. No proof witness is retained as
physical model data.

\subsubsection{Independent cut-family
reuse}\label{independent-cut-family-reuse}

For positive \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{leftWeight}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{rightWeight}}, S11 defines
two boundary coordinates and one interior coordinate with blocks

\begin{verbatim}
KBB = diag(leftWeight, rightWeight)
KBI = (-leftWeight, -rightWeight)^T
KIB = (-leftWeight, -rightWeight)
KII = (leftWeight + rightWeight)
\end{verbatim}

All four generic hypotheses are proved directly, and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{independentBidirectedChainClosure}} calls only
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedSchurDtnClosure}}. The module has no state, next-slot,
provenance-address, M001, or C007 parameter.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-9}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  exact fraction and matrix semantics;
\item
  zero-boundary extension and Laplacian action;
\item
  maximum-defect nonnegativity and positive-arc propagation;
\item
  interior-kernel triviality;
\item
  finite linear existence and uniqueness;
\item
  response existence and witness independence;
\item
  harmonic boundary basis bounds;
\item
  response off-diagonal sign, row conservation, and diagonal
  nonnegativity;
\item
  strict distinguished-port positivity;
\item
  canonical coordinate, matrix, and reachability derivation;
\item
  M003/M004 supporting theorems;
\item
  independent bidirected-chain hypotheses and closure.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-9}

Lean source is split into the aggregate contract module plus S01--S11.
The aggregate import is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CNNAProofs.P001.S11\_IndependentBidirectedChainCutReuse}}, so the
public package includes the independent reuse theorem. The proof build
is pinned to Lean 4.31.0 and mathlib v4.31.0.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-9}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Dropping interior-to-boundary reachability permits a nontrivial
  zero-boundary interior kernel.
\item
  Dropping the distinguished path to another boundary port removes the
  strict-positivity contradiction.
\item
  Replacing row conservation by an approximate identity does not prove
  the exact directed-Laplacian contract.
\item
  Assuming symmetry would exclude the intended directed setting rather
  than prove it.
\item
  Using an inverse or regularizer would change the C006 model and is
  therefore forbidden.
\item
  Verifying only the canonical birth cut would not establish generic
  reuse; the independent bidirected-chain family closes this
  countercheck.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-9}

The exact user-local build completed 26 Core jobs and 8595 proof jobs.
All 142 registered declarations passed
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{P001\_CURRENT\_PROOF\_AXIOM\_AUDIT}};
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FULL\_PACKAGE\_BOUNDARY\_AUDIT}} passed; and the P001 source
emitted no warning. The exact transcript is:

\end{verbatim}

No project-local axiom or \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorryAx}} occurs. The three transitive
Lean/mathlib axioms remain part of the declared trust boundary; their
constructive elimination is not claimed.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-24}

P001 is kernel-verified for all 142 registered declarations. The generic
directed closure is instantiated on the canonical birth-local cut and
independently on a state-free bidirected-chain cut. M003 strict positive
steering and the unique M004 birth-law relation are derived without
changing their Core definitions.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-9}

The result is finite and rational. It does not by itself establish
continuum limits, spectral asymptotics, infinite-volume operator
algebras, or elimination of the transitive Lean/mathlib axioms. Those
claims require separate proof nodes.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-24}

M003 receives a verified positive-domain witness and strict
response-steering theorem. M004 receives existence, uniqueness, and
representative independence for the derived canonical birth law. Later
cuts may reuse \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{directedSchurDtnClosure}} after proving their own
four explicit hypotheses.

\subsection{Open-provenance role: Reusable elimination
theorem}\label{open-provenance-role-reusable-elimination-theorem}

P001 certifies the directed Schur/DtN/Kron specialization independently
of the canonical birth cut. Its generality is mathematical reuse across
admissible finite cuts; it does not identify Schur reduction with other
open-system reductions.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{026 · M004 --- Response-coupled birth law
B\_b}\label{m004-response-coupled-birth-law-b_b}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{026\ ·\ M004}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{M004}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.3.10}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-22}

M004 follows C004, O001, M003, and P001. It constructs one immutable
birth instruction and exposes the exact output boundary consumed by
C008.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-9}

The zero-inclusive pure lift transports one exact nonnegative scalar
over the provenance-determined support. The active law requires strict
positivity. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalM004Closure\ realization}} consumes
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalM003Closure\ realization}} and proves active instruction
existence, uniqueness for each exact pair, and representative
independence.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-25}

The response must determine the birth data through an explicit,
bias-free map while state mutation remains a separate C008
responsibility.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-9}

\begin{verbatim}
parent -> child          sigma
child -> parent          sigma
child -> strict ancestor sigma
older sibling -> child   sigma
child -> older sibling   sigma
birth lapse              sigma
\end{verbatim}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalM004Closure}} obtains a response-steering pair from
M003, derives its positivity from M003, invokes the Core
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{birthLaw}}, and packages the result.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsCanonicalBirthInstructionHandoff}} hides response
representatives and proof witnesses while retaining the Core
instruction.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-9}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  All endpoints are provenance addresses; no self-loop is emitted.
\item
  The direct parent is excluded from the strict-ancestor updates.
\item
  Sibling updates occur in paired orientations and canonical order.
\item
  Every relation value and the lapse use the same exact steering value.
\item
  No state mutation occurs in M004.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-9}

For each exact response-steering pair, the active instruction exists
uniquely. Any two canonical handoffs have
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{BirthInstructionSameValue}}, so representative changes cannot
alter provenance support or exact scalar values.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-9}

The zero lift is exact and annihilates all values but is not an active
C005 birth. Negative values are outside both domains. A root-parent
birth has no strict-ancestor update.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-9}

Python and Core Lean implement the same support and exact scalar
transport. The proof module
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{S02\_CanonicalM004ClosureAndHandoff.lean}} imports only the
closed M003 interface and uses it directly; it does not select a parent
coordinate again.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-9}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Zero remains boundary data and fails active admission.
\item
  Negative or noncanonical inputs are rejected.
\item
  No rank, depth, load, mode, scale, baseline, clipping, fallback, or
  second newborn scalar occurs.
\item
  C008 is not implemented here; the handoff proves M004 output closure
  without claiming state mutation.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-10}

All seven M004 closure and handoff declarations are kernel-compiled and
axiom-audited. Three declaration-level interfaces use \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{propext}}
and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Quot.sound}}; four constructive theorems additionally use
transitive \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Classical.choice}}. No project-local axiom or
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorry}} is admitted.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-25}

M004 has a kernel-verified active-law interface and an immutable C008
handoff without external positivity or parent-index arguments.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-25}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalBirthInstructionHandoff\_exists}} supplies an
instruction; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalBirthInstructionHandoff\_sameValue}} proves
representative-independent output equivalence. C008 alone applies it to
record/live state.

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{KERNEL\_VERIFIED\_EXACT\_SOURCE\_EVIDENCE}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-23}

C008 consumes the immutable canonical birth instruction closed by M004.
It is the deterministic application boundary between the already
verified response/birth calculation and the later construction of a
complete successor state. Its only hard predecessor is M004
(\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{E027:\ M004\ -\textgreater{}\ C008}}).

C008 introduces two derived physics-carrying relation channels:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{record}}: immutable birth-time relation history;
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{live}}: current relation history including later
  response-coupled backreaction.
\end{itemize}

Neither channel is an input parameter. C008 does not recompute M003
response data and does not yet construct the C009 successor
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCapableState}}.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-10}

For existing channels \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(R\_n,L\_n)}} and a canonical M004
instruction \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{B\_n}}, write

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{P(B\_n)}} for \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{parentChildBirthUpdates}};
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{A(B\_n)}} for \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ancestorBackreactionUpdates}};
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{S(B\_n)}} for \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{siblingBackreactionUpdates}}.
\end{itemize}

The C008 update is

\[
R_{n+1}=R_n\mathbin{\|}P(B_n),
\qquad
L_{n+1}=L_n\mathbin{\|}(P(B_n)\mathbin{\|}A(B_n)\mathbin{\|}S(B_n)).
\]

Here \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{\textbar{}\textbar{}}} denotes list/tuple append. The old
channels are exact left prefixes of the new channels. The record
equation contains no ancestor or sibling backreaction term.

At the exceptional bootstrap stage, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bootstrapRecordLiveChannels}}
converts the already-derived C014/C005 bidirected conductances into
exact-fraction updates and initializes \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{record\ =\ live}}. This is
a base-state fact only.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-26}

The M004 output is an immutable birth instruction. A separate
application layer is needed because the same instruction has two
distinct historical effects:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  its direct parent/newborn pair becomes permanent birth record;
\item
  its complete relation support becomes current live response data.
\end{enumerate}

Combining these effects into M004 would mix instruction generation with
state mutation; snapshotting the live state into record would erase
provenance history. C008 isolates exactly this boundary.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-10}

Python defines:

\begin{verbatim}
RecordLiveChannels(record, live)
bootstrap_record_live_channels(X1)
record_instruction_updates(B) = B.parent_child_birth_updates
live_instruction_updates(B) =
    B.parent_child_birth_updates
  + B.ancestor_backreaction_updates
  + B.sibling_backreaction_updates
apply_response_coupled_update(channels, B)
\end{verbatim}

Lean defines the corresponding objects:

\begin{verbatim}
RecordLiveChannels
bootstrapRecordLiveChannels
recordInstructionUpdates
liveInstructionUpdates
applyInstruction
\end{verbatim}

and packages their exact local semantics in
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{RecordLiveResponseCoupledUpdateContract}}.

The implementation contains no independent rank, rank-distance, depth
attenuation, response mode, fitted coefficient, node-load scalar, or
birth bias. Those historical Legacy controls are not migrated into the
current DAG.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-10}

C008 closes the following invariants.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Bootstrap coincidence:}
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bootstrapRecordLiveChannels\ X}} has equal record and live
  lists.
\item
  \textbf{Record prefix preservation:} the previous record is preserved
  exactly and only \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{parentChildBirthUpdates}} are appended.
\item
  \textbf{Live prefix preservation:} the previous live list is preserved
  exactly and the full M004 relation-support delta is appended.
\item
  \textbf{Backreaction separation:} strict-ancestor and sibling
  backreaction never enter the C008 record delta.
\item
  \textbf{No new free update parameter:} the update consumes only
  existing channels and the M004 instruction.
\item
  \textbf{Semantic representative independence:} exact-fraction
  representation choices do not alter the output modulo the established
  SameValue relation.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-10}

M004 proves existence of a canonical handoff instruction and SameValue
equivalence of any two canonical representatives. C008 lifts this
through append.

The core theorem

\begin{verbatim}
applyInstruction_respects_sameValue
\end{verbatim}

proves that SameValue-equivalent old channels updated by
SameValue-equivalent instructions yield SameValue-equivalent output
channels.

The proof facade then combines this with

\begin{verbatim}
canonicalBirthInstructionHandoff_exists
canonicalBirthInstructionHandoff_sameValue
\end{verbatim}

to prove \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalRecordLiveUpdateClosure}}. Literal equality of
raw exact-fraction representations is intentionally not claimed.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-10}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Bootstrap:} record and live coincide only because both
  represent the same first bidirected C014 relation pair.
\item
  \textbf{No ancestor backreaction:} the live delta reduces accordingly;
  record semantics are unchanged.
\item
  \textbf{No sibling backreaction:} likewise.
\item
  \textbf{Empty previous channels:} the output is exactly the
  M004-derived deltas.
\item
  \textbf{Nonempty previous channels:} all old entries remain exact
  prefixes; no historical entry is rewritten.
\item
  \textbf{Different exact-fraction representatives:} output equality is
  SameValue, not structural fraction equality.
\end{itemize}

C008 does not define behavior beyond the M004 instruction domain and
does not prove the full successor-state schema.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-10}

The Python and Lean layers implement the same structural equations but
serve different evidential roles.

Python source:

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{derivation/code/python/src/cnna/derivation/s01\_primitive\_response\_coupled\_finite\_provenance/s04\_record\_live\_update\_backreaction\_channels\_and\_finite\_closure/s01\_c008\_\_record\_live\_response\_coupled\_update.py}}

Lean Core:

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{derivation/code/lean/core/src/CNNA/Derivation/S01\_PrimitiveResponseCoupledFiniteProvenance/S04\_RecordLiveUpdateBackreactionChannelsAndFiniteClosure/S01\_C008\_RecordLiveResponseCoupledUpdate.lean}}

Lean proof facade:

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{derivation/code/lean/proofs/src/CNNAProofs/C008/S01\_CanonicalRecordLiveUpdateClosure.lean}}

Python uses immutable tuples and normalized \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Fraction}} values.
Lean uses lists of M004 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{DirectedRelationUpdate}} values and C006
exact-fraction SameValue semantics. The cross-layer claim is semantic
agreement of the update partition, not byte-level data-structure
identity.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-10}

\begin{verbatim}

\end{verbatim}

Evidence file:

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{derivation/registry/documentation/C008\_PYTHON\_REGRESSION\_20260808.txt}}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist

\item

\item

\item

\item

\item

  partition.
\end{itemize}

The 99-case sweep is \textbf{finite evidence}, not a universal proof.
The universal structural result is the Lean theorem/contract.

The historical Legacy code was used only to identify qualitative
hypotheses worth retesting. Its free coefficients, node-load mutation,
rank-distance factors, alternative schedules, and mode-dependent update
laws are not copied into C008.

\subsection{Infobox --- SameValue Is the Correct Equality
Boundary}\label{infobox-samevalue-is-the-correct-equality-boundary}

M004 can admit distinct exact-fraction representations of the same
rational values. Requiring literal structural equality in C008 would
strengthen the upstream contract without justification. C008 therefore
preserves exactly the equivalence M004 proves: endpoint equality plus
exact-fraction SameValue entry by entry. This keeps the update canonical
without smuggling in a representation-normalization postulate.

\section{028 · C016 --- Immutable record
channel}\label{c016-immutable-record-channel}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{028\ ·\ C016}}\\

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-24}

C016 consumes the kernel-verified C008 record/live update and isolates
its birth-time record projection. C009 consumes C016 together with C017
and the C005 state schema.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-11}

For arbitrary existing C008 channels and one admissible canonical M004
instruction \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{B}}, C016 closes
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{record\textquotesingle{}\ =\ record\ ++\ B.parentChildBirthUpdates}}.
The previous record is therefore a literal left prefix. Ancestor and
sibling backreaction do not occur in the record delta. Exact-fraction
representation changes are compared only through the established C006
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{SameValue}} relation.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-27}

C008 already carries both channels, but downstream nodes need the
historical record as a separately named construction boundary. Keeping
C016 separate prevents later live information from being retrospectively
written into provenance history.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-11}

Python exposes \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{immutable\_record\_channel}} and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{record\_channel\_after\_instruction}}. Lean exposes
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{recordChannel}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{afterInstruction}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{afterInstruction\_eq\_append}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{previousRecord\_isLeftPrefix}}, and
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{afterInstruction\_respects\_sameValue}}, packaged by
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ImmutableRecordChannelContract}}.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-11}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Bootstrap record equals the already-derived bootstrap relation pair.
\item
  Every one-step update preserves the complete previous record literally
  as a left prefix.
\item
  The only appended record block is the direct parent/newborn M004 birth
  pair.
\item
  Strict-ancestor and sibling backreaction never enter the C016 record
  suffix.
\item
  The projection respects C006/M004 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{SameValue}}.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-11}

No independent C016 choice is made. The projection is definitionally
determined by C008, and its representative independence is inherited
from \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{applyInstruction\_respects\_sameValue}}.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-11}

The bootstrap coincidence of record and live is not promoted to a later
identity. Empty and nonempty old records are both covered. C016 does
\textbf{not} prove arbitrary-many-future-step invariance because the
typed recurrent successor chain has not yet been constructed.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-11}

Python uses immutable tuples; Lean uses lists. The semantic claim is the
same append-only partition, not byte-level data-structure identity.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-11}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{114\ tests,\ 1086\ subtests\ PASS}}. C016-specific tests check

tuple, and type rejection. This is finite regression evidence only.

\section{030 · C009 --- Codomain state
Xₙ₊₁}\label{c009-codomain-state-xux2099ux2081}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{030\ ·\ C009}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{C009}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.4.4}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D1}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}\\
\textbf{Formal state:}
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{KERNEL\_VERIFIED\_EXACT\_SOURCE\_EVIDENCE}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-26}

C009 is the first merge point between the C005 recurrent state schema
and the already kernel-verified C016/C017 channel projections. It
consumes the canonical C004 next slot as carried by the M004
instruction. The next theorem node is T002, which must show that the raw
C009 codomain re-enters C005.

\subsection{Mathematical Contract}\label{mathematical-contract-13}

For one \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCapableState\ X}}, one \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{NextOpenSlot\ X}},
coherent current record/live channels, and one
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCoupledBirthInstruction}} typed at that slot, C009
assembles the unique raw codomain data

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{(schedule,\ bornNonRoot,\ record,\ live)}}

with:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{schedule\ =\ X.schedule}};
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bornNonRoot\ =\ X.bornNonRoot\ ++\ {[}next.val{]}}};
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{record\ =\ C016.afterInstruction\ channels\ instruction}};
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{live\ =\ C017.afterInstruction\ channels\ instruction}}.
\end{enumerate}

The C005↔C017 boundary is represented by \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{StateChannelCoherent}}:
the pre-step live channel must be
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{DirectedRelationUpdatesSameValue}} to the ordered C005
conductance list after the already-defined exact-fraction representation
map.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-29}

C016 and C017 intentionally stop at their channel projections. C005, by
contrast, packages the state-level carrier and conductance invariants.
C009 is therefore needed as a non-dynamical assembly boundary before
T002 can ask whether one response-coupled step closes back into the same
state schema.

\subsection{Explicit Construction}\label{explicit-construction-13}

Lean defines \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CodomainAssemblyInput}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CodomainStateData}},
and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{assemble}}. Python exposes the corresponding frozen
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CodomainStateData}} and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{assemble\_codomain\_state\_data}}.
No response scalar, rank force, depth attenuation, schedule policy, new
coefficient, or Legacy node-load rule is introduced in C009.

\subsection{Invariants}\label{invariants-13}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  The canonical schedule is inherited literally.
\item
  The born non-root prefix changes by exactly one append of the
  C004-selected child.
\item
  The record field is exactly C016; C009 defines no second record law.
\item
  The live field is exactly C017; C009 defines no second live law.
\item
  The pre-step C017 live channel must represent the current C005
  conductance list.
\item
  C006/M004 representative changes are propagated only through
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{SameValue}}.
\item
  No full C005 schema-closure field is constructed inside C009.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Canonicity Or Uniqueness}\label{canonicity-or-uniqueness-13}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsCodomainAssembly\ input\ output}} is the extensional
specification \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{output\ =\ assemble\ input}}.
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{codomainAssembly\_existsUnique}} proves, without a mathlib
dependency in the Core, that one fixed admissible input has exactly one
raw codomain output. The earlier \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{∃!}} parser issue was only
notation; the verified source uses the equivalent explicit
existential-plus-uniqueness form.

\subsection{Boundary Cases}\label{boundary-cases-13}

An incoherent C005/C017 pre-state is rejected by the Python boundary
check and is not an admissible \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CodomainAssemblyInput}} in Lean.
An instruction at a non-canonical slot is likewise rejected in Python;
Lean types the instruction at the supplied \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{NextOpenSlot}}. C009
does not claim that every raw output satisfies
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bornWithinCutoff}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bornOrdered}}, \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{bornInitial}},
conductance support/positivity/ordered-pair uniqueness, or
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{parentBackbone}}. Those are T002 closure obligations, with any
missing supporting lemma repaired at its semantic owner.

\subsection{Python Lean Cross Layer}\label{python-lean-cross-layer-13}

Both layers explicitly assemble the same four components: schedule, born
prefix, record and live. Python uses tuples and can attempt a finite
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCapableState}} realization for regression. Lean keeps the
raw codomain separate from the C005 structure so that the universal
schema-closure theorem cannot be smuggled into a constructor.

\subsection{Countercheck}\label{countercheck-13}

The finalized Python suite reports
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{117\ tests,\ 1086\ subtests\ PASS}}. C009-specific tests verify
exact C004/C016/C017 component assembly, rejection of an incoherent
C005↔C017 handoff, and small finite realizations of the candidate as a
C005 state. The realization sweep is explicitly
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FINITE\_REGRESSION\_EVIDENCE\_ONLY}}; it is not the T002 theorem.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-29}

The retained build transcript SHA-256 is

Axiom-profile counts over the eight audited declarations are:

There is no project-local axiom and no \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorryAx}}.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-29}

C009 supplies the deterministic raw codomain to T002. T002 is now
kernel-verified and closes the load-bearing recurrent C005 re-entry
together with post-step C005↔C017 live coherence. The next active node
is T003; C010 is dependency-ready from C009+C018 but remains later in
canonical order.

\section{031 · T002 --- Recurrent state-closure
theorem}\label{t002-recurrent-state-closure-theorem}

\textbf{Canonical node label:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{031\ ·\ T002}}\\
\textbf{Semantic ID:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{T002}}\\
\textbf{Current section path:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{1.4.5}}\\
\textbf{Documentation tier:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{D2}}\\
\textbf{Documentation state:} \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{COMPLETE\_V2}}\\
\textbf{Formal state:}
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{KERNEL\_VERIFIED\_EXACT\_SOURCE\_EVIDENCE}}

\subsection{Position In Derivation}\label{position-in-derivation-27}

T002 is the load-bearing merge theorem after C009. C009 assembles the
raw one-step codomain, while T002 proves that the response-coupled step
re-enters the complete C005 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ResponseCapableState}} schema and
restores the C005↔C017 live-channel interface needed for another step.

\subsection{Formal Statement}\label{formal-statement-10}

For every C005 state \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{X}}, canonical \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{NextOpenSlot\ X}}, and
derived \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{RecordLiveChannels}} whose live component is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{StateChannelCoherent\ X\ channels}}, the C007 canonical
state-directed realization and the already-closed M003/M004 response
chain determine a proof-bearing recurrent input. Its
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{successorState}} satisfies the complete C005 schema, is the
unique recurrent successor for that fixed input, and the updated C017
live channel is coherent with the successor conductance list.

\subsection{Hypotheses}\label{hypotheses-10}

The explicit recurrent context is exactly: (1) a valid C005 state, (2)
its proof-bearing C004 next slot, and (3) the derived record/live
history with pre-step C005↔C017 coherence. No C007 realization, response
representative, steering value, positivity witness, rank force, or
additional coefficient is a free T002 input.

\subsection{Introduction Reason}\label{introduction-reason-30}

Before T002 the derivation had a raw codomain assembly but no universal
theorem that every admissible step remained inside the C005 state space.
Without that closure, later finite iteration would be a sequence of
unverified constructor attempts rather than a theorem-supported
recurrence.

\subsection{Proof Strategy}\label{proof-strategy-10}

The proof is split by semantic ownership. Facts that first arise inside
C004, C005, C006, M001, C007, or M004 are proved in origin-local closure
modules. T002 then performs only the cross-interface work:
exact-fraction live updates are realized as rational conductances, the
old and new ordered pairs are shown disjoint, the conductance list and
parent backbone are extended, the full successor state is constructed,
and post-step live coherence is derived.

\subsection{Lemma Chain}\label{lemma-chain-10}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  C004 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{successorBornPrefixClosure}} preserves cutoff, non-root,
  ordering and initialization facts under the selected child append.
\item
  C006 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{ExactFraction.toRat\_*}} closes representative equality
  and positive-rational realization.
\item
  M001 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{PortSupportClosure}} separates and de-duplicates the
  canonical port support.
\item
  C007 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{canonicalStateDirectedBlockRealization}} constructs the
  rational block realization internally.
\item
  M004 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{LiveRelationDeltaClosure}} closes pairwise support, born
  endpoints and child-touching structure of the complete live delta.
\item
  T002 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{realizedLiveDelta\_sameValue}},
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{successorConductances\_pairwise}},
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{successor\_parentBackbone}}, and
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{successor\_live\_coherent}} perform the merge-specific closure.
\item
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{recurrentSuccessor\_existsUnique}} and
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{RecurrentStateClosure}} package successor uniqueness and the
  raw-codomain/live-coherence handoff.
\item
  The proof facade derives the canonical M003/M004 witnesses and exports
  \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{CanonicalRecurrentStateClosureContract}}.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Formal Realization}\label{formal-realization-10}

\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{RecurrentStepInput}} contains channels, the coherence proof,
response, exact steering value, response-steering relation and
positivity proof. It intentionally contains no
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{StateDirectedBlockRealization}} field. \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{successorState}}
constructs the new C005 state; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{IsRecurrentSuccessor}} is
extensional equality with that state; \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{RecurrentStateClosure}}
packages raw-codomain agreement, unique recurrent successor and
post-step live coherence. The public proof facade derives the numerical
realization and M003/M004 witnesses instead of exposing them as public
parameters.

\subsection{Counterexamples Or Necessity
Checks}\label{counterexamples-or-necessity-checks-10}

The Python regression contains finite full-iteration checks and rejects
incoherent pre-step live channels. These checks are evidence against
accidental schema drift but are not the universal proof.
Architecturally, dropping pre-step \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{StateChannelCoherent}} would
sever the identification between the current C005 conductance state and
the C017 live history; exposing a free C007 realization would
reintroduce a parameter that C007 already derives. Both are therefore
guarded explicitly by the static boundary audit.

\subsection{Axiom Profile}\label{axiom-profile-12}

The user-local Lean 4.31.0 run audits 26 declarations. Counts are:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist

\end{itemize}

The only observed transitive axioms are \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{propext}},
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Classical.choice}}, and \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{Quot.sound}}. There are 0
project-local axioms and 0 \texttt{\protect\seqsplit{sorryAx}} occurrences in the accepted
path.

\subsection{Result}\label{result-30}

The 2026-08-09 user-local build completes the 35-job Core and the
8611-job T002 proof target, reports
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{T002\_CURRENT\_PROOF\_AXIOM\_AUDIT\ PASS}}, sets
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{t002\ =\ KERNEL\_VERIFIED\_CURRENT\_BUILD}}, sets
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{t002\_recurrent\_state\_closure\_olean:\ true}}, preserves every
predecessor exact-source hash gate, and ends with
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{FULL\_PACKAGE\_BOUNDARY\_AUDIT\ PASS}}.

The retained transcript SHA-256 is
\texttt{\protect\seqsplit{5f86f1acc7011823925154d261f9cf10f6f93755c27675f82e69c402532f4249}}.

\subsection{Remaining Limits}\label{remaining-limits-10}

T002 is a one-step recurrent closure theorem. It does not yet prove
exhaustion of the finite BFS schedule, label-equivariance/no-rank-bias,
the complete finite carrier theorem, cumulative response time, or later
continuum/AQFT claims. Those remain assigned to T003, C010/C011/C012,
P004-P006 and C019 or later sections.

\subsection{Downstream Handoff}\label{downstream-handoff-30}

T002 certifies one-step iterability for C019. In canonical derivation
order the next active node is T003, whose task is to prove that
sibling-slot relabeling introduces no independent hard-coded rank force
while preserving the transported event/response history.
